# Current game progress

Goal: ordinary-rule autonomous progression toward Factorio major objectives (rocket launch). No spawning, teleporting, speed changes or free research. Active Codex goal exists.

Tools: `python3 play.py` reads current snapshot; `send '<JSON>'` or `file filename.json` submits ordinary character commands through native Unicode key events and waits for request-ID acknowledgement. Keyboard console emits a won/backslash character on this Mac: client deletes it before entering `/astra ...`. Clipboard paste is unreliable; do not use it. `native-input key 53` is Escape. Screen inspection uses CUA `app` Factorio.

Mod source `mods/astra-control_0.1.0/control.lua`; installed copy under ~/Library/Application Support/factorio/mods/astra-control_0.1.0. To update, copy source then `/astra {"op":"reload"}`. This uses game.reload_mods without restarting the app or breaking OBS window capture. `/c game.reload_mods()` was used once to bootstrap reload and disabled achievements (user informed and accepted). No items were created by this command.

Snapshots: ~/Library/Application Support/factorio/script-output/astra/state.json. Audit: audit.jsonl. `/astra scan` writes charted local entities/tiles to scan.json; `placements` queries legal positions using actual inventory cursor within build distance, writes placements.json. `mine` walks into normal reach automatically, then mines normally. Other actions still require reach. Explicit walk targets may hit obstacles, so use safe waypoints, tolerance=1, and verify logs. Action exceptions clear the pending queue. Crafting queue continues normally after a command error. Missing mine targets finish as exhausted.

Game world:
- Coal looping burner drills ID355 (-18,-52) east and ID356 (-16,-52) west. Take fuel inventory coal but leave some to keep running.
- Iron burner ID287 (40,-102) south -> furnace ID288 (40,-100).
- Additional iron burner (43,-105) south -> furnace (43,-103); burner (46,-108) south -> furnace (46,-106).
- Copper burner (-24,-72) south -> furnace (-24,-70).
- Electric power: pump ID743 (-26.5,40.5) south, boiler ID744 (-26,38.5) east, steam-engine ID745 (-22.5,38.5) east. Connected and working, boiler fuel recently refilled.
- Red science: lab ID746 (-17.5,35.5); science assembler ID785 (-13.5,35.5); gear assembler ID786 (-9.5,35.5). Copper chest ID787 (-13.5,32.5), iron chest ID788 (-9.5,32.5). North input inserters direction0, between-assembler and lab output inserters direction4. These directions are correct and verified running. Initial science feed 100 iron/29 copper. Poles at (-20.5,36.5), (-14.5,32.5), (-8.5,32.5), (-15.5,37.5), (-11.5,37.5). Bottom poles are necessary to power the horizontal inserters.
- Researched: steam-power, electronics, automation-science-pack, automation. Current electric-mining-drill research consumes 25 red science and is being fed automatically (verify latest snapshot).
- Trees: most of cluster around (-66,-164) mined for 20 wood. More trees around (-94,-130) and (-75,-165). Stone field (-40,-66), nearby cliffs/rocks vary.
- Empty crash wrecks near (-22,0), (-32,-5); main crash ship (-5,-6) obstructs direct north/south path. Safe routes to mines via (10,15)->(10,-80), or west (-42,25)->(-42,-45).

Next intended work: refuel smelters, fetch copper/iron, extend electrical line to mines and build electric drills once research finishes; then logistics/steel/green science. Proposed pole line from existing (-20.5,36.5) north every 7 tiles to (-20.5,-96.5), east every 7 tiles to (49.5,-96.5). 29 new poles; 3 in inventory plus crafting 13 times makes 26 (cost 13 wood and 13 copper). Collision around crash wreck at (-22,0) may require normal mining. Initial electric iron drills could be (43.5,-99.5), (50.5,-99.5) south, each into adjacent furnaces; check actual legal placement.

Latest save `_autosave-astra-progress.zip`; original preserved as astra-start.zip. Save regularly with action `save`, name astra-progress.

OBS: user requested 60% Factorio / 40% Codex. Scene factorio is configured left 1152×1080 and right 768×1080 on 1920×1080 canvas, bounds scale-inner and crops zero. Source named misspelled Korean Factorio uses current Factorio window; macOS screen capture source uses Codex. Native windows sized 1024×960 and 683×960 using window-layout.swift, matching aspect ratios. Avoid restarting Factorio since its OBS window ID changes. User authorized resume after layout completed.

Update (Sep 9): Navigation now uses Factorio `surface.request_path` and normal walking_state, with no pathing through owned entities or destructive pathing. Transfers and out-of-range builds automatically walk into reach. Mine also uses pathfinding when needed. Construction can still fail if the character stands in the proposed footprint; move away and retry.
